}

  "command": "cat > /tmp/debug_gold_steps.py << 'EOF'\nimport json, csv\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ntraj_index = {}\nwith open(\"/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/data/swe_agent_trajectories_5000.jsonl\") as f:\n    for i, line in enumerate(f):\n        traj_index[i] = json.loads(line)\n\nwin_index = {}\nwith open(\"/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/data/windows_labeled.jsonl\") as f:\n    for line in f:\n        w = json.loads(line)\n        wid = w[\"trajectory_id\"] + \"__w\" + str(w[\"window_idx\"])\n        win_index[wid] = w\n\ngold_labels = {}\nwith open(\"/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/artifacts/gold_set/gold_2plus_agreed_B.csv\") as f:\n    reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    for row in reader:\n        gold_labels[row[\"window_id\"]] = int(row[\"gold_label\"])\n\nreasons = []\nfor wid in gold_labels:\n    w = win_index[wid]\n    traj = traj_index.get(w[\"row_index\"])\n    if traj is None:\n        reasons.append(\"no_traj\")\n        continue\n    trajectory = traj.get(\"trajectory\", [])\n    if not trajectory:\n        reasons.append(\"empty_traj:\" + str(list(traj.keys())[:5]))\n        continue\n    valid_steps = [trajectory[si] for si in w[\"step_indices\"] if si < len(trajectory)]\n    if not valid_steps:\n        reasons.append(\"OOB:len=%d,idx=%s\" % (len(trajectory), str(w[\"step_indices\"][:3])))\n    else:\n        # Check if steps have text\n        has_text = any(s.get(\"text\") for s in valid_steps)\n        reasons.append(\"ok\" if has_text else \"no_text\")\n\nprint(Counter(reasons))\nEOF"
}

  "command": "bash -c 'source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 << \"PYEOF\"\nimport json, csv\n\n# Check OH gold window row_indices and step_indices\nwin_index = {}\nwith open(\"/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/data/windows_labeled.jsonl\") as f:\n    for line in f:\n        w = json.loads(line)\n        wid = w[\"trajectory_id\"] + \"__w\" + str(w[\"window_idx\"])\n        win_index[wid] = w\n\n# Load OH trajectories\noh_traj = {}\nwith open(\"/root/autodl-tmp/openhands_data/openhands_trajectories.jsonl\") as f:\n    for i, line in enumerate(f):\n        oh_traj[i] = json.loads(line)\n\n# Check a few OH gold windows\nwith open(\"/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/artifacts/gold_set/gold_2plus_agreed_B.csv\") as f:\n    reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    oh_ok = 0\n    oh_oob = 0\n    for row in reader:\n        wid = row[\"window_id\"]\n        if \"openhands\" not in wid:\n            continue\n        w = win_index.get(wid)\n        if w is None:\n            continue\n        ri = w[\"row_index\"]\n        traj = oh_traj.get(ri)\n        if traj is None:\n            oh_oob += 1\n            continue\n        tlen = len(traj.get(\"trajectory\", []))\n        max_idx = max(w[\"step_indices\"])\n        if max_idx >= tlen:\n            oh_oob += 1\n        else:\n            oh_ok += 1\n    print(f\"OH windows: ok={oh_ok}, oob={oh_oob}\")\nPYEOF'"
}

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   Run LLM Judge experiment on the gold set (505 windows, conf≥2) using `claude-sonnet-4-20250514` with two modes: zero-shot and 5-shot-CoT. Compare results against structural GB gold baseline (0.839 macro_F1). The experiment ID is `llm-judge-gold-r5`. This is for a reviewer response requiring a non-circular LLM gold baseline. Steps: (1) understand gold set data and existing LLM judge code, (2) write the script, (3) run via submit_training_job on GPU 1, (4) record results and register artifacts.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - OpenRouter-compatible proxy at `http://47.94.22.126/v1` for Claude API access
   - Model name on proxy: `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4` (maps to claude-sonnet-4-20250514)
   - Fernet-encrypted secrets in `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513/config/secrets/`
   - Decryption via `core.crypto.load_secret()` with PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 derived from machine MAC address
   - Gold set has windows from TWO agents: SWE-agent (336) and OpenHands (169)
   - Two separate trajectory files needed for step extraction
   - Concurrent API calls with ThreadPoolExecutor (15 workers)
   - Resume support via JSON checkpoint files (saved every 100 results)
   - Server uses conda environment: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/exp_llm_judge_gold.py`** (on server)
     - Main experiment script created and patched during this session
     - Loads gold labels from CSV, windows from JSONL, trajectories from two JSONL files
     - Runs zero-shot and few-shot-cot modes with concurrent API calls
     - Key function signatures after patching:
       - `extract_window_steps(window, traj_index_swe, traj_index_oh)` - determines agent type from trajectory_id
       - `select_few_shot_examples(train_windows, traj_index_swe, traj_index_oh, n_per_class=3)` - selects 3 stagnation + 2 progress examples
       - `run_mode(client, model, mode, gold_windows, traj_index_swe, traj_index_oh, few_shot_text, output_path, max_workers=15)`
     - Default args: `--model anthropic/claude-sonnet-4`, `--base_url http://47.94.22.126/v1`, `--max_workers 15`
     - Patched via `/tmp/patch_llm_judge.py` Python script that did string replacements to add dual trajectory support
   
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/artifacts/gold_set/gold_2plus_agreed_B.csv`**
     - Gold set: 505 windows (372 progress/label=1, 133 stagnation/label=0, stag_rate=26.3%)
     - Format: `window_id,gold_label`
   
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/data/swe_agent_trajectories_5000.jsonl`**
     - SWE-agent trajectories (5000), indexed by line number (row_index)
     - Each line: JSON with keys `instance_id, model_name, target, trajectory, exit_status, generated_patch, eval_logs`
   
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/openhands_data/openhands_trajectories.jsonl`**
     - OpenHands trajectories (5000), same format, separate file
   
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/data/windows_labeled.jsonl`**
     - All windows (214,239 total), keys: `trajectory_id, instance_id, row_index, window_idx, step_indices, short_trajectory, labels, majority_label, confidence`
   
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe/checkpoints/split_info.json`**
     - Train/test split indices used for selecting few-shot examples (train only)
   
   - **Existing reference scripts**: `llm_judge_strengthened.py`, `llm_judge_oh.py`, `evaluate_gold_set.py`
     - Used for understanding data loading patterns, prompt templates, and dual trajectory handling

   - **API Key**: Decrypted from `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513/config/secrets/agent_progress_probe/openrouter_api_key.enc` → `tum_XXB3sZILRCg8llb3NjxVbbRNzFpsYVFdiogFkavpGn8`

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Model name not found (503 error)**: Proxy doesn't have `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-20250514`. Listed models via `client.models.list()`, found available: `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5`, `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4`, `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6`. Fixed by using `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4`. Verified with test API call that returned correct model behavior.
   
   - **sed didn't apply**: First `sed -i` appeared to work but the running job still used old model name. Fixed by explicitly passing `--model anthropic/claude-sonnet-4` on the command line.

   - **104/505 gold windows had no trajectory steps (OOB)**: Root cause: gold set includes 169 OpenHands windows whose `row_index` maps to `openhands_trajectories.jsonl`, NOT `swe_agent_trajectories_5000.jsonl`. The script only loaded SWE-agent trajectories. Found this by: (1) checking `evaluate_gold_set.py` which uses `--oh_trajectories` parameter pointing to `/root/autodl-tmp/openhands_data/openhands_trajectories.jsonl`, (2) verifying all 169 OH windows have valid steps in OH file, all 336 SWE windows have valid steps in SWE file. Fixed by writing a Python patch script (`/tmp/patch_llm_judge.py`) that updated `extract_window_steps()`, `select_few_shot_examples()`, `run_mode()`, and `main()` to accept and use dual trajectory indices. Verified: `ok=505, fail=0` after patch.
